- **godot-review**: Review a Godot project for structure, architecture, maintainability, and risk. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/godot-review.md) · ★110 · 4 copies)

- **godot-review**: 완성된 Godot 게임을 UX/게임디자인/기술 3관점으로 병렬 리뷰. 코드를 수정하지 않고 평가만 수행. ([vp-k/godot-craft](https://github.com/vp-k/godot-craft/blob/HEAD/commands/godot-review.md) · ★1)
- **godot-runtime-verify**: Verify a built Godot 2D or 3D scene at runtime: run it, read the debugger output, classify errors against a Godot taxonomy, decide a PASS/FAIL gate. Real output is the evidence. Use after a Godot slice is implemented, to gate runtime behavior. Do not use to plan a scene (use godot-scene-plan), write or fix code (use implement-approved-slice or implement-slice-complement), triage a failure (use incident-triage), or with no scene to run. ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/godot-runtime-verify.md) · ★10)
- **godot-scaffold**: Godot 프로젝트 스캐폴딩 + 에셋 생성. Phase 1만 단독 실행. ([vp-k/godot-craft](https://github.com/vp-k/godot-craft/blob/HEAD/commands/godot-scaffold.md) · ★1)
- **godot-scene**: 创建 Godot 场景文件: $ARGUMENTS ([skyoxu/godotgame](https://github.com/skyoxu/godotgame/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/godot-scene.md) · 4 copies)
- **godot-scene-audit**: Resolve to the shared `commands/godot-scene-audit.md` workflow. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/godot-scene-audit.md) · ★110)
- **godot-scene-audit**: Audit Godot scenes, nodes, autoloads, and scene composition patterns. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/godot-scene-audit.md) · ★110 · 4 copies)
- **godot-scene-plan**: Plan the Godot scene and node structure for a 2D or 3D game feature before GDScript is written: scene tree, nodes, autoloads, signals, input map. A 3D plan SHALL declare its renderer tier. Do not use for game-idea framing (use problem-framing in its game-design mode), slicing a plan (use implementation-plan), blast radius (use impact-analysis), scene verification (use godot-runtime-verify), or with no task folder (run task-init). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/godot-scene-plan.md) · ★10)
